ReferenceError and failure log

Error and failure log

1181 cases from the “Troubleshooting” sections of all written modules, following the pattern symptom → cause → what you see in the log or on the instrument → consequences → fix. Collected automatically when the site is built; to add a case, extend the troubleshooting section of a module. Breakdowns of real incidents are in the module “Real crash case studies”.

FoundationsOperatorElectronics and radioКонструкторIntegratorState estimation and navigationAutonomyEngineer: code on boardPerception and intelligenceElective: INAVElective: PX4Elective: Mathematical foundationsElective: ROS 2 on board

Foundations

The drone flips over immediately on takeoff

Cause: the motor order or spin direction is wrong, the props are mounted the wrong way round.

Fix: without props, check each motor against the frame diagram, check the props.

How a multirotor flies
The drone slowly rotates in yaw while hovering

Cause: a tilted motor, mismatched props, motors of different strength.

Fix: check the motor alignment, fit identical new props.

How a multirotor flies
Hover at more than 60–70% throttle

Cause: overload: 60 % throttle means T/W 2.25–2.8, 70 % — 1.8–2.0 (the “Hover throttle” table).

Fix: make it lighter, fit different props/motors, don't fly in wind.

How a multirotor flies
In Angle mode the drone loses altitude in a sharp bank

Cause: T = m·g/cos φ exceeds the available thrust; T/W is low or the tilt limit is large.

Fix: limit the angle, enable tilt throttle compensation (Angle/Horizon only; starting point and check — the table in the theory), increase T/W.

Flight mechanics: forces, moments, equations of motion
A large aircraft “doesn't complete” yaw, the I-term winds up

Cause: the reaction torques are small, I_yaw is large.

Fix: lower your expectations of yaw rates, revisit the yaw PID, tilted motors on large frames.

Flight mechanics: forces, moments, equations of motion
After moving the battery to the edge of the frame the response became sluggish and oscillations appeared

Cause: the moment of inertia grew and the center of mass shifted.

Fix: mass — toward the center, retune the PID.

Flight mechanics: forces, moments, equations of motion
The hover current in the log is much higher than calculated

Cause: wrong mass, a table for a different voltage, damaged props, wind.

Fix: reweigh, check against the table for your voltage, inspect the props.

Flight mechanics: forces, moments, equations of motion
The multimeter burned out / the fuse blew during a measurement

Cause: the probe in the A jack while measuring voltage.

Fix: replace the fuse; rule: after measuring current, move the probe back to V immediately.

Electricity and measurements 101
One battery cell is 0.1 V or more below the others

Cause: cell degradation, imbalance.

Fix: balance charge; if it happens again — the battery goes to storage/disposal, not into the air.

Electricity and measurements 101
The XT60 is hot after a flight

Cause: contact resistance: I²·R.

Fix: replace the connector, check the soldering.

Electricity and measurements 101
Flight time is half of what the capacity calculation gives

Cause: calculated from the rating, the real capacity is lower (age, cold), the current is higher.

Fix: measure the delivered capacity with the charger, take the average current from the log.

Electricity and measurements 101
Motor phase resistances differ

Cause: an inter-turn short or a damaged wire.

Fix: replace the motor; check whether it heats up more than the others.

Electricity and measurements 101
A low-voltage warning on every punch

Cause: sag under current, an old battery with high internal resistance.

Fix: look at the rest voltage after the flight, measure the internal resistance with the charger.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
Flight time much shorter than calculated

Cause: the average current is higher than it seemed, or the battery has lost capacity.

Fix: take the average current from the log, check the capacity with a charge cycle.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
The VTX or camera reboots on throttle

Cause: sag on the BEC rail or power from the wrong pad.

Fix: check the power pad, add a capacitor on the power bus, check the BEC current.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
The battery is warm and swollen after the flight

Cause: current overload.

Fix: take the battery out of service, reconsider capacity/C for the real current.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
A spark and hot wires when connecting a battery to a parallel board

Cause: different voltages or a different number of cells among the batteries on the board.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the cell checker shows a difference of more than 0.1 V per cell.

Fix: disconnect, check each battery with a cell checker, equalize them one at a time in Storage mode, replace the board's blown fuses.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
A retired battery shows voltage again after “discharging”

Cause: the discharge was stopped too early (charger to 3.0 V/cell, an LED bulb, salt water).

Fix: a resistive load down to ~0 V, check with a multimeter after an hour without a load.

Power: LiPo, Li-Ion, BEC
One motor is hotter than the others

Cause: a bearing, rubbing, a damaged winding or prop.

Fix: compare the rotation of the bells by feel, swap the suspicious motor with another and see whether the problem “moved”.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
A motor twitches or stops on a sharp throttle input

Cause: ESC desync.

Fix: check the timing/demag settings in the ESC firmware, the phase soldering.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
When the battery is connected the ESC is silent or doesn't play its tune

Cause: no power to the ESC, a burned channel, no protocol selected.

Fix: check the power soldering, MOTOR_PROTO in the arming flags, the ESC firmware.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
The RPM filter doesn't enable

Cause: ESC firmware without bidirectional DShot or a wrong motor_poles.

Fix: Bluejay or AM32, recount the magnets.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
A motor twitches/screeches on a sharp throttle input

Cause: desync — demag/timing, long wires, a prop too heavy for the KV.

Fix: first raise demag, then timing by one step (watch the motor temperatures), check the phase wires; if that doesn't help — a smaller prop (in detail — f-esc-firmware).

Motors, ESCs and DShot
E% on the Motors tab is above zero

Cause: bidirectional DShot errors — ESC firmware, noise on the signal, DShot speed.

Fix: update the ESC (Bluejay/AM32), DShot300 instead of 600, shorter signal wires.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
Thrust “drops off” after a minute of flight

Cause: ESC or motor thermal protection.

Fix: the ESC temperature from telemetry, cooling airflow, a lower PWM frequency, the current from the log.

Motors, ESCs and DShot
Hover at more than 60% throttle

Cause: T/W too low — the prop is too small, the KV is low, or the mass has grown.

Fix: recalculate from the table, a prop of larger diameter/pitch, reduce the mass.

Propeller aerodynamics and power system selection
The motors are hot after a short flight

Cause: the prop is too big or has too much pitch for the KV — the current is above the continuous rating.

Fix: less pitch or diameter, check the hover current in the log.

Propeller aerodynamics and power system selection
Flight time is half of what was calculated

Cause: the calculation was for static hover, but the flight had maneuvers; or the battery capacity is below the rating.

Fix: the average current from the log, check the capacity with a cycle.

Propeller aerodynamics and power system selection
It won't take off in the mountains, though it flew on the plains

Cause: lower air density — less thrust at the same RPM.

Fix: a prop of larger diameter/pitch or reducing the mass.

Propeller aerodynamics and power system selection
The model in the configurator rotates the wrong way

Cause: the board is mounted rotated, and the rotation is not set in the firmware.

Fix: set board alignment / AHRS_ORIENTATION.

Flight controller and sensors
NOGYRO in the arming flags

Cause: the gyro is not detected — the wrong firmware (target) or a damaged board.

Fix: reflash with the correct target, check the board for damage.

Flight controller and sensors
The compass heading “drifts” with throttle

Cause: magnetic interference from the power wires.

Fix: move the compass with the GPS onto a mast, twist the power wires.

Flight controller and sensors
The baro altitude jumps in hover

Cause: airflow from the props hitting the sensor.

Fix: cover the barometer with foam.

Flight controller and sensors
The receiver on a UART does not work on F4

Cause: SBUS on a port without an inverter.

Fix: a port with an inverter (labeled) or a protocol without inversion (CRSF).

Flight controller and sensors
Blackbox cuts off/has gaps

Cause: a DMA conflict or slow flash.

Fix: lower the logging rate, disable the conflicting function (LED), SD instead of flash.

Flight controller and sensors
The FC reboots when GPS+VTX+camera are connected

Cause: the 5 V/9 V BEC current is exceeded.

Fix: add up the currents, move a consumer to a separate BEC.

Flight controller and sensors
A peak in the spectrum does not move when throttle changes

Cause: a resonance of the frame or the FC mount.

Fix: arm/standoff stiffness, a damper, a dynamic notch at that frequency.

Frame, mechanics and vibration
The peak at the motor frequency is huge

Cause: prop or motor imbalance.

Fix: balance, replace the prop, check the bell.

Frame, mechanics and vibration
VIBE and Clip grow in ArduPilot

Cause: a hard FC mount, imbalance of large props.

Fix: FC damping, balancing, wires without tension.

Frame, mechanics and vibration
After fitting dampers the flight became “mushy”

Cause: a mount that is too soft, a low-frequency resonance.

Fix: stiffer grommets, less mass on the damper.

Frame, mechanics and vibration
After a throttle punch the aircraft dips its nose

Cause: I cannot keep up, anti-gravity is too low.

Fix: raise anti_gravity_gain, check thrust_linear.

PID controller in Betaflight
The I term is visible in the log as a rise after a flip and extra rotation

Cause: I-term relax is off or the cutoff is high.

Fix: iterm_relax = RP, cutoff 10–15.

PID controller in Betaflight
dyn_idle_min_rpm = 30, but propwash and desync in falls have not changed

Cause: bidirectional DShot is not working, and the dynamic idle target is silently zeroed (mixerInitProfile()); or the value is set in a different PID profile.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the header motorOutput:158,2047 instead of 48,2047; all the DYN_IDLE debug channels are 0.

Fix: dshot_telemetry_info, get dshot_bidir, profile and get dyn_idle_min_rpm in the profile you fly (bf-rpm-filter).

PID controller in Betaflight
After dynamic idle is enabled the aircraft is “floaty”, brakes flips poorly, and has short inverted hang time

Cause: the dyn_idle_min_rpm target is too high for these motors and props.

Fix: lower it in steps of 5 (500 RPM) and compare the logs of the fall.

PID controller in Betaflight
D shake on the ground disappears only on the first takeoff after power-up; after landing and arming again on the same pack it returns

Cause: the low-throttle TPA (tpa_low_*) is turned off after the first pass of tpa_low_breakpoint — even if the throttle was raised before arming — and returns only after a reboot (which is what changing the pack does).

Fix: set tpa_low_always = ON; in parallel — the D filters (bf-filters).

PID controller in Betaflight
Oscillations only at full throttle, clean in hover

Cause: TPA is too weak for this setup: at high throttle the open-loop gain and the noise grow.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: in 2025.12 debug_mode = TPA [0] — the factor × 1000 (350 at full throttle with the defaults).

Fix: first lower tpa_breakpoint (1350 → 1250), then raise tpa_rate (65 → 80): at full throttle the factor becomes 0.20 instead of 0.35.

PID controller in Betaflight
On Li-Ion after vbat_sag_compensation = 100 the aircraft is noticeably weaker in a punch-out at the start of the flight

Cause: vbat_warning_cell_voltage was lowered to 3.00 V, the compensation range became 1.20 V, and 28.6 % of the top is cut on a full pack.

Consequences: less thrust in a punch-out, and the top of the range moves with the sag under throttle.

Fix: compensation 0 or a smaller percentage; do not choose the warning threshold to suit the compensation.

PID controller in Betaflight
The hover throttle is slightly higher than usual after thrust_linear is enabled

Cause: the throttle compensation is approximate: with a hover at 0.30 and thrust_linear = 40 the output is 0.316 (+5.4 %).

Fix: this is expected; if it bothers you, reduce thrust_linear to 20 (+1.5 %).

PID controller in Betaflight
The drone won't arm

Cause: an arming disable flag is active.

Fix: status in the CLI or the OSD Warnings; the table above; fix the cause.

Safety and the preflight check
The drone armed right after the battery was connected

Cause: arming is assigned to the wrong channel or range; or the link already existed before the battery was connected (the receiver was powered from USB), and NOT_DISARMED was not set — it is set only at the moment the link appears with ARM raised (a conclusion from the code of fc/core.c).

Fix: check Modes, the ARM channel range, add PREARM.

Safety and the preflight check
After arming the aircraft jerked sideways and disarmed

Cause: runaway takeoff — swapped motors/prop directions.

Fix: a motor test without props, the direction diagram, resource/motor remap.

Safety and the preflight check
After a crash the battery heats up without a load

Cause: an internal short.

Fix: isolate it on a non-flammable surface outdoors, watch it, do not charge it, dispose of it.

Safety and the preflight check
The link has returned, but the ArduPilot aircraft doesn't respond to the sticks and flies home

Cause: this is normal — after a radio failsafe Copter stays in RTL (visible in lab 1).

Fix: flip the mode switch to exit RTL; practice this in SITL before your first flight.

Safety and the preflight check
A Betaflight aircraft armed on the bench does not disarm by itself

Cause: auto_disarm_delay works only with MOTOR_STOP, stick arming and no Airmode; with switch arming Betaflight only beeps, while the motors spin at motor_idle.

Fix: disarm with the switch; on the bench — only without props.

Safety and the preflight check
The pack is hot after a flight, the charger won't start charging or complains about the temperature

Cause: the pack is outside the 10–45 °C window (Gens Ace datasheet), the charger's temperature sensor tripped.

Fix: let it cool in the shade on a non-flammable surface; do not cool it with water or put it in a fridge (condensation).

Safety and the preflight check
The VTX is hot, no video

Cause: it was powered without an antenna.

Fix: always attach the antenna before power; the VTX may already be damaged.

Safety and the preflight check
It is not clear whether registration is needed

Cause: the threshold mass differs by country and changes.

Fix: look only at the official source, write down the date of the check.

Rules, zones and operator responsibility
The aircraft is under 250 g — “so the rules don't apply”

Cause: the 250 g threshold applies only to some of the requirements, and not in every country.

Fix: USA — no registration only for recreational flights, under Part 107 registration regardless of mass; United Kingdom — a Flyer ID already from 100 g; EU — operator registration if there is a camera. Check against the official page.

Rules, zones and operator responsibility
I fly in goggles alone

Cause: only an observer next to the pilot satisfies the visual line of sight requirement.

Fix: an observer for every FPV sortie (USA, United Kingdom, EU); the role is described in the ops-field module.

Rules, zones and operator responsibility
The site “seems clear”, but there is an aerodrome nearby

Cause: aerodrome zones often extend for kilometers.

Fix: the official zone map; if in doubt — don't fly.

Rules, zones and operator responsibility
An incident involving third parties

Cause: any.

Fix: help the injured, record what happened, notify according to the country's rules, the log.

Rules, zones and operator responsibility
The flight-time calculation doesn't converge: the battery grows, the time doesn't

Cause: the mass → current → mass loop diverges; the aircraft class is too small for the mission.

Fix: a bigger prop/frame or lower the requirements.

Choosing components for the task and budget
T/W is 3 on paper, but hover is above 60 % throttle (for T/W 3, 50–58 % is expected, f-physics)

Cause: a thrust table for a different voltage or prop; the real mass is higher.

Fix: reweigh, recalculate from your own table, check the voltage under load.

Choosing components for the task and budget
Not enough UARTs for all the peripherals

Cause: the FC was chosen without counting the ports.

Fix: an FC with more UARTs or combining functions (for example, ESC telemetry on one port).

Choosing components for the task and budget
The budget is exceeded twofold

Cause: props, hardware, the charger, spare parts were not accounted for.

Fix: a 15–20% margin in the budget, postpone the optional (digital video) to the next iteration.

Choosing components for the task and budget
A new whoop does not link with the radio although the binding phrase is the same

Cause: an SPI receiver under Betaflight 2025.12 is ELRS 3, while the radio is already on 4.x.

Fix: check the version in ELRS Lua and the receiver type; the options are in the table “The ELRS version trap” and in link-elrs.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
A black screen in the DJI goggles, the air unit is working

Cause: an incompatible generation (Goggles N3 and O3; Goggles V2 and O4) or the air unit is not bound to the goggles.

Fix: check the pair against the matrix, bind again, update the firmware on both sides.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
The kit is bought, but you get to fly 4 minutes per session

Cause: a single battery.

Fix: calculate the number of batteries for the goal (step 4) and the charger power for them.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
The charger charges four batteries in two hours instead of one

Cause: the built-in power supply is weaker than the power needed, and the charger reduced the current by itself.

Fix: P = U × I for the whole bundle; an external power supply or fewer batteries at a time.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
The battery does not connect to the charger or to the aircraft

Cause: different connectors (XT30 and XT60; BT2.0, A30 and PH2.0 on 1S).

Fix: an adapter for charging; on the aircraft change the connector by soldering, not with a chain of adapters.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
The RTF kit is mastered, and a new BNF does not bind to the kit's radio

Cause: the kit's radio works on its own protocol or has no ELRS.

Fix: an EdgeTX radio with ELRS; keep the kit's radio for the simulator.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
A “sub-250” aircraft showed 262 g on the scales

Cause: the battery is heavier than calculated, or a camera or mounts were added.

Fix: weigh it with the battery, take a battery of smaller capacity or fly by the rules of your category (f-regulations).

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
A used radio does not update to EdgeTX 2.12

Cause: the MCU is an STM32F2 — supported only in the 2.11 branch.

Fix: install 2.11.8; for Arm using = Switch in ELRS 4, EdgeTX 2.11.0+ is enough.

Первый комплект: пульт, очки, аппарат и бюджет
The built-in receiver stopped linking after the radio was updated, the phrase is correct

Cause: the radio is on ELRS 4.x, while the SPI receiver on Betaflight 2025.12 speaks OTA version 3.

Fix: put the module back on 3.x, fit an external receiver over a UART, or build Betaflight 2026.6 with ELRSV4. Flashing the “receiver” through ExpressLRS Configurator is useless: its firmware does not exist.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
LAND NOW after a minute of flight, and after landing the cell shows 3.8 V

Cause: sag across the PH2.0 connector or an old cell, not discharge.

Fix: calculate as in step 2, switch to BT2.0/A30, measure the cell resistance, retire the worn ones. Lower the thresholds as a last resort.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
A fresh LiHV is connected, but the OSD does not show the cell count and gives no alarms

Cause: 4.35 V is above vbat_max_cell_voltage = 430, so the battery is considered not connected.

Fix: set vbat_max_cell_voltage = 440, and optionally force_battery_cell_count = 1.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
The whoop falls in a flip or on a sharp throttle cut

Cause: the motor loses sync at low throttle: a low motor_idle, dynamic idle does not work without dshot_bidir.

Fix: raise motor_idle in steps of 50–100, enable dshot_bidir, check the Bluejay version (f-esc-firmware).

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
After enabling the RPM filter the motors are hot and the noise in the log is still there

Cause: motor_poles = 14 (the default) or 9 on a 12-pole motor.

Fix: count the magnets, set the number, compare the RPM in the Motors tab.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
The video of a digital whoop breaks up on throttle

Cause: the 1S voltage sags below the VTX operating voltage (for the O4 Lite — below 3.5–3.7 V).

Fix: a 5 V step-up BEC, a BT2.0/A30 connector, fresh cells.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
A series charging board, and the charger shows fewer cells than are inserted

Cause: a cell is not pushed fully into the slot or is deeply discharged.

Fix: do not start the charge, check each cell separately.

Вупы и микро: 1S/2S, AIO, SPI-приёмники и разъёмы
The requirements sound like wishes (“reliable”, “fast”)

Cause: no criterion and no verification method.

Fix: rewrite with a number and a test; if there is no number — it is a goal, not a requirement.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
After tuning it is unclear whether things got better or worse

Cause: no metric and no “before” log.

Fix: before a change — a log and a metric; an entry in the change log with the result.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
A month later it is unclear why a parameter has this value

Cause: a change without a record.

Fix: the rule “no line in the CHANGELOG — no change”.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
Someone else cannot reproduce the build

Cause: the README describes a dream, not the aircraft.

Fix: the README — the current state, firmware versions, the config in the repository.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
The requirement is met on the stand but not in the field (T/W, flight time)

Cause: the requirement has no conditions; the stand measured on a full battery and in cool air.

Fix: add the voltage and temperature to the requirement, recalculate with the corrections (step 6) and compare by the guard-band rule.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
Reprocessing the same log gives a different number

Cause: processing by hand in a spreadsheet, a modified script, or a random method without a seed.

Fix: the script in the repository, its commit in the record, a fixed seed, sha256sum of the logs; manual cell edits are forbidden.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
After a field trip “it got better”, but it is unclear why

Cause: several things were changed during one trip.

Fix: one change per experiment; roll back to the baseline (a git tag) and introduce the changes one at a time.

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
The most expensive instrument was upgraded, but the total uncertainty did not change

Cause: a small contribution of the budget was improved.

Fix: sort the budget by share of variance and reduce the top row (in the example the scales are 0.2 %).

Engineering Method 101: requirements, architecture, tests, documentation
The configurator doesn't see the board

Cause: the driver (Windows), a charge-only cable, the board is in DFU.

Fix: Zadig for DFU, a different cable, check the COM port.

Tools: Configurator, GCS, git
I don't remember what I changed before it broke

Cause: no “before/after” diffs.

Fix: the rule “always a file before changes,” git.

Tools: Configurator, GCS, git
Python libraries conflict between projects

Cause: everything is installed into the system Python.

Fix: one venv per project.

Tools: Configurator, GCS, git

Как научиться летать: симулятор, акро и первые сто батарей
The configurator does not see the controller, the port does not appear

Cause: the drivers are not installed (Windows), a poor USB cable, a port conflict.

Fix: replace the cable with one that has data lines. On Windows install the drivers with ImpulseRC Driver Fixer. Restart the computer. Try another USB port.

Introduction to Betaflight
The controller connects, but the 3D model does not react to movement

Cause: the accelerometer is not calibrated, the sensor is damaged.

Fix: calibrate the accelerometer on a level surface. If that does not help — check status in the CLI and make sure ACCEL is detected. Check the wires and the solder joints.

Introduction to Betaflight
The drone does not arm, an error is blinking in the top right corner (for example, RXLOSS)

Cause: the receiver is not getting a signal, the port is not set up, a wrong protocol.

Fix: check that the correct UART is selected for Serial RX and the right protocol is selected (CRSF/SBUS). Make sure the radio is on and the receiver is linked (the indicator on the receiver is lit). In the Receiver tab check that the bars move.

Introduction to Betaflight
The motors spin, but the drone does not take off or flips when it tries to

Cause: a wrong motor rotation direction, a wrong connection order, a wrong FC orientation.

Fix: on the Motors tab check the motor order (Motor 1 — rear right, Motor 2 — front right and so on). Check the rotation direction (by the marking on the propellers). Make sure the FC rotation angle (Yaw Degrees) is set correctly.

Introduction to Betaflight
The sticks move in the configurator, but the values do not reach 1000–2000

Cause: the endpoints on the radio are set incorrectly.

Fix: in the radio settings adjust Travel/Endpoints to 1000–2000 µs. Alternatively use the CLI command rxrange for correction.

Introduction to Betaflight
The drone arms, but the motors do not spin (with MOTOR_STOP off)

Cause: the motor idle is too low for startup.

Fix: 2025.12 has no min_throttle variable (4.5 had it, but it was not used with DShot): on the Motors tab raise Motor Idle (motor_idle, before 2025.12 — dshot_idle_value; 5.5% by default).

Introduction to Betaflight
After flashing the receiver/GPS do not work although the wiring is correct

Cause: the feature is not included in the Cloud Build.

Fix: status → the BUILD KEY, and with it on build.betaflight.com — the list of build options (version prints only the version, target, date and git revision); reflash with the ones you need.

Introduction to Betaflight
LOAD in the arming flags or CPU > 90%

Cause: an F4 at 8 kHz with all the features.

Fix: pid_process_denom = 2, disable LED/unneeded telemetry, tasks to find the heavy ones.

Introduction to Betaflight
After the ports are saved and the FC reboots, the settings “roll back”.

Cause: a conflict of functions on one UART (Serial Rx + an extra MSP and the like) or the limit of MSP on three ports.

Fix: in the Ports tab leave only what is needed on the receiver's UART; make sure there are no more than three ports with MSP; save and reboot.

Ports and receiver
The bars in Receiver do not move, and the receiver is linked.

Cause: Serial Rx is on the wrong UART; a wrong Provider; firmware without the required protocol (Cloud Build).

Fix: check Ports and Configuration; for BF 4.4+ rebuild the firmware with the required receiver protocol.

Ports and receiver
CRSF/ELRS works, but there is no telemetry on the radio.

Cause: telemetry is off in the link settings, or the provider is not CRSF.

Fix: Serial Receiver Provider = CRSF; enable telemetry per the ELRS/radio manual.

Ports and receiver
SBUS on an F4 does not work.

Cause: no inverter, the signal does not go to the SBUS pin / the wrong UART.

Fix: the SBUS pin on the board, a change of protocol to CRSF (or FPort for a FrSky receiver), an external inverter.

Ports and receiver
The bars move but do not fit within 1000–2000.

Cause: endpoints on the radio; rxrange is not set.

Fix: set the travel on the radio; if needed, `rxrange` in the CLI.

Ports and receiver
After GPS passthrough was set up, the MSP settings “disappear”.

Cause: the limit of three ports with MSP is exceeded.

Fix: disable MSP where it is not needed; leave no more than three ports with MSP.

Ports and receiver
After Ports is saved the configurator “drops out”

Cause: MSP is disabled on the USB/VCP port, or MSP is enabled on a 4th port.

Fix: connect through another port/DFU, serial VCP 1 115200 … (in 4.5 — serial 20 1 115200 …) for VCP.

Ports and receiver
ESC Sensor gives no telemetry

Cause: the wire is on TX instead of RX, the ESC has no telemetry, esc_sensor is not enabled.

Fix: the RX pin, the ESC firmware, feature ESC_SENSOR in the CLI (or bidirectional DShot as an alternative for RPM).

Ports and receiver
The receiver is bound, but the channels don't move in Receiver

Cause: TX/RX are swapped or the wrong UART is selected in Ports.

Fix: check the crossover soldering and the UART number per the board schematic.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
SBUS on F4 doesn't work on any port

Cause: connected to a UART without an inverter.

Fix: find the pad with the inverter on the schematic or use the receiver's uninverted output.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
Channels work, but there's no telemetry on the radio (CRSF)

Cause: the controller TX → receiver RX wire isn't soldered, or half-duplex is enabled.

Fix: check the second wire and serialrx_halfduplex = OFF.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
The sticks “twitch” in place

Cause: noise on the line, a bad GND or the wrong speed.

Fix: check the common GND, move the wire away from the power lines.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
Channels arrive, but the FC failsafe doesn't trigger when you turn off the radio

Cause: the receiver “holds” the last values, the SBUS failsafe flag isn't being read, or CRSF isn't set to “cut”.

Fix: set the receiver to “no pulses”/cut or failsafe values; check the FC's reaction on the ground without props.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
LQ in the OSD is 100%, but RSSI dBm isn't shown

Cause: the OSD element is set to channel-based RSSI rather than CRSF Link Statistics.

Fix: enable the “RSSI dBm”/“Link quality” element (Betaflight: osd_link_quality_pos, osd_rssi_dbm_pos), disable rssi_channel.

SBUS, CRSF and UART inversion
Arming does not work, the OSD shows ARM_SWITCH (before 2025.12 — ARMSWITCH) or NOPREARM

Cause: the ARM switch was on when power was applied, or PREARM is not on.

Fix: turn ARM off and on again, check PREARM.

Modes and OSD
A mode is turned on by the wrong switch

Cause: the channel or the range is mixed up.

Fix: the check in the Modes tab; the Receiver tab — which channel moves.

Modes and OSD
No OSD in the goggles

Cause: OSD is not enabled in Configuration, the wrong type (analog/digital), the UART is not set up for DisplayPort.

Fix: check the OSD type and Ports.

Modes and OSD
The voltage warning comes too late

Cause: the threshold was calculated for the resting voltage.

Fix: raise the threshold to account for the sag under load.

Modes and OSD
A mode “flickers” at a switch boundary

Cause: a range without a margin, channel jitter.

Fix: ranges with a 100 µs margin, check in the Receiver tab.

Modes and OSD
The OSD elements “moved” after the switch to HD

Cause: the positions were set for the analog canvas.

Fix: arrange them again for the HD canvas (get osd_canvas); an OSD profile will not help here — the position is shared by the three profiles.

Modes and OSD
An element is enabled in the OSD tab but is missing in the goggles, or disappears at the flick of a switch

Cause: the profile bit of the active profile is not set (bits 11–13), or adjrange changed osd_profile.

Fix: get osd_profile, in the OSD tab enable the element for all the profiles needed; check the value with osd_decode().

Modes and OSD
After a manual set osd_*_pos = … the element ended up somewhere else or disappeared

Cause: x > 31 was written without splitting it into bits 0–4 and 10; the index 53 × y + x from the application was pasted; the profile bit is shifted by one.

Fix: recalculate with the formula from the theory; the check — osd_decode() returns the intended x, y and profiles.

Modes and OSD
After a diff was carried from 4.5 to 2025.12 the LQ warning disappeared, but RSSI or OVER_CAP appeared

Cause: the osd_warn_bitmask line was carried over as a number; in 2025.12 RC_SMOOTHING was removed and bits 9–18 moved down by one.

Fix: enable the warnings again in the OSD tab of the application of the same version as the firmware (Betaflight App for 2025.12; Configurator 10.10 labels the bits by the 4.5 list — its bit 8 is RC_SMOOTHING), or recalculate the mask from the 2025.12 table.

Modes and OSD
set display_name = … or set name = … from an old backup gives INVALID NAME

Cause: these are 4.3 variables; from 4.4 they are called pilot_name and craft_name.

Fix: replace the names in the backup, check get craft_name.

Modes and OSD
After a failsafe the drone does not arm, the OSD shows NOT_DISARMED

Cause: the ARM switch was left on.

Fix: turn ARM off and on again after failsafe_recovery_delay.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
In AUTO-LAND the drone tumbles or drifts off to the side

Cause: the accelerometer is disabled or not calibrated (ANGLE in Stage 2 is enabled automatically only with an ACC), or failsafe_throttle is not tuned.

Fix: set up Channel Fallback Settings / rxfail for the ANGLE channel.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
In AUTO-LAND the drone climbs

Cause: failsafe_throttle is above hover throttle (for example, after a change of battery or props).

Fix: recalculate the hover throttle from a fresh log.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
RXLOSS / failsafe in ordinary flight

Cause: a weak link (the receiver antenna, the packet rate, TX power).

Fix: look at RSSI/LQ in the log, see the module on ExpressLRS.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
When the radio is turned off the aircraft keeps flying on the last stick positions

Cause: on link loss the receiver holds the last values and does not report the loss (neither “no pulses” nor a failsafe flag in the SBUS frame) — Stage 1 does not start.

Fix: configure the receiver's failsafe (ELRS over CRSF always goes silent; for an SBUS output No Pulses and Last Position are both suitable — in the second case the frame carries the failsafe flag, which Betaflight reads; PWM/PPM — only “no pulses”; FrSky — select it), and test without props.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
GPS Rescue does not fly home / “goes crazy”

Cause: home was recorded with a poor fix, there is no compass and too little speed for a GPS heading, magnetic interference.

Fix: arm after a fix with 8+ satellites, the compass on a mast, check gps_rescue_sanity_checks.

Failsafe: stages and switch test
After the signal returns, control does not come back

Cause: failsafe_recovery_delay + the requirement to move the sticks (failsafe_stick_threshold).

Fix: deflect a stick > the threshold; reduce recovery_delay moderately.

Failsafe: stages and switch test

Пульт на EdgeTX: модели, миксы, телеметрия и модули
RXLOSS when turning with the back toward the pilot

Cause: the receiver antenna is shadowed by the frame or carbon fiber.

Fix: move the antenna out, orient it at an angle, check it for damage.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ
LQ “sags” in waves even though the drone is close

Cause: strong 2.4 GHz interference (Wi-Fi, other pilots) or a damaged radio antenna.

Fix: try D250/D500 mode or 900 MHz, check the TX antenna.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ
Won't bind after an update

Cause: different major ELRS versions or different binding phrases.

Fix: update both sides to the same version, check the phrase.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ
F1000 isn't available

Cause: an SPI receiver or insufficient UART speed on the radio.

Fix: use a serial receiver and check the module baud rate.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ
LQ 100%, but control is jerky

Cause: low packet rate with high rc_smoothing, or telemetry is too frequent.

Fix: 250 Hz, look at debug_mode = RC_SMOOTHING, ratio 1:64.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ
Range in the field is half the calculated value

Cause: antennas (polarization, carbon), onboard interference, Dynamic Power doesn't keep up.

Fix: the “Antennas” and “Interference” modules; fixed power for the test; LQ/RSSI to the log.

ExpressLRS: packet rate, telemetry, LQ

Lua-виджеты EdgeTX: свой экран телеметрии
The OSD shows one frequency, but the goggles get the picture on another

Cause: FACTORY mode, and the FC table doesn't match the VTX's built-in table.

Fix: load the table made specifically for your VTX model and your region.

VTX tables and changing the video channel
The VTX doesn't respond to channel changes from Betaflight

Cause: the UART isn't configured for SmartAudio/Tramp, or there is no table.

Fix: check Ports (VTX on the right UART) and that a vtxtable exists.

VTX tables and changing the video channel
A neighbor gets interference after the channel change

Cause: frequency spacing too small, or harmonics at high power.

Fix: increase the spacing, lower power on the ground (pit mode).

VTX tables and changing the video channel
Channel “E5” is a different frequency for you and your neighbor

Cause: the VTXs have different band tables.

Fix: agree on frequencies in MHz.

VTX tables and changing the video channel
The VTX won't change channels from the OSD

Cause: the protocol isn't responding (SmartAudio version/wiring), the VTX is locked (pit/lock).

Fix: CLI vtx_info — is there a response from the device; wiring to the TX pin; unlock it following the VTX's instructions.

VTX tables and changing the video channel
One pilot's picture gets “snowy” when a second one takes off

Cause: adjacent channels with overlapping bandwidth, or too much power.

Fix: spacing ≥ 37 MHz, the minimum sufficient power, a field frequency table.

VTX tables and changing the video channel
make TARGET=SITL stops with arm-none-eabi-gcc not in the PATH

Cause: mk/tools.mk checks for the ARM toolchain for any target.

Fix: make arm_sdk_install or a dummy arm-none-eabi-gcc script in the PATH that prints the required version (10.3.1).

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
Betaflight App or the CLI see INAV instead of Betaflight (MSP_FC_VARIANT = INAV)

Cause: INAV SITL is running on the same machine and uses the same TCP 5760–5761.

Fix: close it or run Betaflight SITL in a separate network namespace (unshare -n, bring up lo).

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
In SITL arming fails with the ANGLE flag

Cause: there is no FDM data, and the horizon is not estimated (isUpright() requires attitudeIsEstablished).

Fix: feed the FDM of a stationary aircraft to UDP 9003 or connect a simulator.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
The RC stream is stopped, but RXLOSS does not appear

Cause: in SITL the frame status is always “received” after the first packet.

Fix: check failsafe with the FAILSAFE mode on a switch, and link loss — only on hardware.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
At the first liftoff the aircraft flips or spins in place

Cause: the motors are not in QUADX order, the direction is wrong, or yaw_motors_reversed does not match the props.

Consequences: broken props, frame, sometimes an ESC; on the bench this is caught in 5 minutes.

Fix: remove the props, a test in the Motors tab, motor_output_reordering, dshotprog/Motor direction.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
After dshotprog the direction did not change

Cause: the protocol is not DShot, the aircraft is armed, or command 12 was not sent.

Fix: DShot, disarm, dshotprog <i> 8 12, reconnect the battery and check.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
The voltage in the OSD differs from the multimeter by 0.3–1 V

Cause: vbat_scale is from another board or was corrected the wrong way.

Fix: new = old × V_multimeter / V_FC; increasing scale increases the reading.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
The mAh in the OSD is 10–30 % less than the charger puts back

Cause: ibata_scale is too high (the current is inversely proportional to scale).

Fix: new = old × mAh_FC / mAh_charger, a control flight.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
PWM ESCs: one motor starts later than the others

Cause: the ESCs are not calibrated to 1000/2000 µs.

Fix: calibration with the master slider without props; move to DShot if the ESC supports it.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
After a real failsafe GPS Rescue disarms and the aircraft falls

Cause: there was no home point, too few satellites, or ap_hover_throttle (before 2025.12 — gps_rescue_throttle_hover) is too low.

Fix: arm only with a home point, the hover throttle from the log, tests from a switch with STAGE1 at a short distance.

Betaflight с нуля: от пустой платы до первого висения
The aircraft does not arm, the MSP flag in Betaflight App

Cause: the app itself blocks arming while it is connected.

Fix: Disconnect; per the documentation, pulling the USB cable does not clear the flag. Check arming without the app.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
It arms on the bench but not in the field (or the other way around)

Cause: ANGLE on uneven ground (> 25°), MSP/CLI on the bench, GPS without satellites in the field.

Fix: read the flag in the OSD with ARM on; do not raise small_angle instead of finding a level spot.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
There are no flags in status, but the switch does not arm

Cause: status was taken with ARM off; the ARM range on the Modes tab does not match the real channel value; ARM is on and ARM_SWITCH (in 4.5 — ARMSWITCH) is still set after an earlier reason.

Fix: turn ARM on and look at Setup/OSD; check on Receiver that the channel value is no closer than 50 µs to the range boundary; lower the switch and raise it again.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
When the throttle is raised the aircraft flips instantly and disarms, beeping 1 long + 1 short

Cause: RUNAWAY — the motors, the rotation direction, the props or the board orientation are mixed up.

Consequences: without the protection — a flip within 0.3 s, broken props, a risk to the pilot.

Fix: take the props off, item 2 of the practice, the Setup tab (does the model follow the tilt?); do not turn runaway_takeoff_prevention off.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
A RUNAWAY disarm on the bench without props

Cause: the aircraft was moved by hand with the motors armed, or a long yaw with the stick (the 50 °/s threshold).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: debug_mode = RUNAWAY_TAKEOFF, debug[1] = 1 for 75 ms before the disarm.

Fix: do not hold an armed aircraft in your hands; for long bench tests — the Motors tab.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
CALIB does not go away

Cause: the aircraft is rocking or vibrating (a fan, the table), the gyro standard deviation > gyro_calib_noise_limit.

Fix: place it motionless; do not raise the limit until you are sure the gyro is healthy.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
DSHOT_TELEM after enabling dshot_bidir

Cause: an ESC without bidir firmware (BLHeli_S without Bluejay) or one ESC is not responding.

Fix: dshot_telemetry_info, flash Bluejay/AM32 or dshot_bidir = OFF (bf-rpm-filter).

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
After clipping a gate the aircraft spins in yaw and climbs

Cause: a yaw spin; with yaw_spin_recovery = OFF or a threshold of 1950 the recovery kicks in late.

Fix: AUTO or ON with a threshold 100–200 °/s above max yaw; throttle to zero, sticks to center, and with a prolonged spin of more than 0.5 s — disarm.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
The aircraft bounces or flips over after touchdown on landing

Cause: airmode is latched, and the PID holds level with the props in the ground.

Fix: disarm right after touchdown; landing_disarm_threshold (from 2025.12) does this on impact.

Почему не армится и как не улететь: флаги арма, runaway takeoff, yaw spin recovery
No OSD in the goggles, video is fine

Cause: the UART isn't in MSP / DisplayPort mode, TX/RX are swapped, the HD OSD device isn't selected.

Fix: check the port function, osd_displayport_device, the TX/RX crossover wiring.

Digital FPV video: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero, OpenIPC
The picture freezes at range without warning

Cause: the digital “cliff”, flying at the limit of link quality.

Fix: show video link quality in the OSD, turn back at a threshold, check the antennas and polarization.

Digital FPV video: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero, OpenIPC
Blocks and “mush” over grass and trees

Cause: the bitrate is too low for a scene with lots of motion.

Fix: a higher-bitrate mode, lower resolution or frame rate, a cleaner spectrum.

Digital FPV video: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero, OpenIPC
Interference for a neighbor at the field

Cause: wide digital channels next to analog ones, a wrong frequency plan.

Fix: agree on channels, lower power on the ground, separate by frequency.

Digital FPV video: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero, OpenIPC
Latency is noticeably higher than advertised

Cause: a low-frame-rate mode, weak signal, recording in the goggles, different firmware.

Fix: measure in different modes, update the firmware, check which mode is actually active.

Digital FPV video: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero, OpenIPC
There is video, but no OSD in the goggles (question No. 11)

Cause: the port mask is 1 or 131072 instead of 131073; vcd_video_system is not HD; the OSD is off; TX/RX are not crossed over; the port is SoftSerial.

Fix: serial → the mask 131073 on the air unit's UART, get vcd_video_system, feature OSD, continuity checks; the decision tree is below.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
After saving Ports the receiver disappeared, and the ports went back to defaults

Cause: VTX_MSP without MSP or a fourth port with MSP — isSerialConfigValid() reset the whole port config.

Fix: validate() from the practice, then fix the mask and check the number of ports with MSP (≤ 3).

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
set displayport_msp_serial = 3 → INVALID NAME

Cause: the variable was removed in 4.4, and the instructions were written for 4.3.

Fix: nothing needs to be entered — the port is determined by the mask 131073.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The OSD has moved into the top left corner, or elements overlap each other

Cause: a layout from SD (30×16) or from another HD canvas; osdInit() only clamps to the edge.

Consequences: voltage covered by mAh cannot be seen in flight.

Fix: a layout for your own canvas (get osd_canvas), a separate diff per system.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
Some OSD elements or warnings are not drawn, the rest are in place

Cause: displayport_msp_fonts = 0,1,2,3 with a single-page font in the goggles.

Fix: the color Betaflight font in the goggles, or set displayport_msp_fonts = 0,0,0,0.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
On the first connection of the goggles the flight controller rebooted by itself

Cause: the goggles sent MSP_SET_OSD_CANVAS while the FC was not in HD/MSP — the firmware switched, saved and rebooted.

Fix: nothing, this is normal; check that get vcd_video_system = HD.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The air unit shuts down or the recording is cut off on the bench

Cause: overheating without airflow; for the O4 a cold start without airflow is about 2.5 min.

Fix: a fan, do not keep it powered on the ground; HDZero — 0MW instead of P1MW.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The HDZero VTX did not boot (no blue LED) with USB connected

Cause: the battery was connected to a flight controller already connected to a PC.

Fix: first the battery, then USB; VTX power within the specification.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The DJI radio is linked, but the bars in Receiver do not move

Cause: the yellow S.Bus wire is not on the S.Bus pad (an F4 without an inverter), serialrx_provider is not SBUS.

Fix: the S.Bus pad, Serial RX on that UART, serialrx_provider = SBUS (bf-ports).

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
Your own logo is gone, and the standard one is back at power-up (analog)

Cause: another font was uploaded — “Upload Font” rewrites all 256 characters, including 160–255 with the logo.

Fix: select the font, substitute the 288 × 72 picture again, Upload Font; keep your own .mcm with the logo.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The Font Manager is grayed out, or “Select custom image…” refuses

Cause: the OSD device is MSP (HD) or the MAX7456 was not detected; the picture is not 288 × 72 px or has pixels outside the three colors (anti-aliasing, a PNG with a palette of shades).

Fix: for HD the font is changed in the goggles; for analog check status (the OSD type), and export the picture without anti-aliasing strictly in 0,255,0 / 0,0,0 / 255,255,255.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
On an analog aircraft there is no OSD or it jitters (question No. 12)

Cause: the build has “OSD (HD)”, and the default is HD/MSP; the wrong video system for the camera.

Fix: set vcd_video_system = PAL (or NTSC/AUTO), osd_displayport_device = MAX7456 or AUTO, centering with vcd_h_offset/vcd_v_offset.

Цифровое видео на Betaflight: DJI, Walksnail, HDZero — подключение, OSD и настройка
The modems won't link (green LED blinking)

Cause: mismatched NETID, AIR_SPEED, ECC, frequency range or number of channels.

Fix: ATI5 and RTI5, make them match, AT&W, ATZ on both.

Telemetry radios: SiK, RFD900, ELRS MAVLink, IP radios and LTE
The link is up, but the GCS gets no data

Cause: the modem's SERIAL_SPEED doesn't match the flight controller port's baud rate, the port isn't set to MAVLink, TX/RX are swapped.

Fix: check the port baud rate and protocol (SERIALn_BAUD, SERIALn_PROTOCOL) and the wiring.

Telemetry radios: SiK, RFD900, ELRS MAVLink, IP radios and LTE
Telemetry drops out at a few hundred meters

Cause: low fade margin, antennas with different polarization or shadowed by the airframe, interference from the video transmitter or a nearby GNSS.

Fix: RSSI/noise from RADIO_STATUS, vertical antennas on both sides, move the antenna out from under the frame, lower AIR_SPEED.

Telemetry radios: SiK, RFD900, ELRS MAVLink, IP radios and LTE
Parameters and missions take very long to load

Cause: a narrow channel (low AIR_SPEED, ECC enabled), the channel is busy with streams.

Fix: turn ECC off, load on the ground, lower stream rates (MAVn_* in ArduPilot 4.7, numbered from 1: MAV1 is the former SR0; before 4.7 — SRn_*).

Telemetry radios: SiK, RFD900, ELRS MAVLink, IP radios and LTE
Two vehicles see each other's telemetry

Cause: the same NETID.

Fix: a unique NETID for each pair, different vehicle sysids (MAV_SYSID, before 4.7 — SYSID_THISMAV; in PX4 — MAV_SYS_ID).

Telemetry radios: SiK, RFD900, ELRS MAVLink, IP radios and LTE
It is calm at the ground, but at altitude the aircraft cannot get back

Cause: the wind grows with height, the limit was calculated from the wind at the ground.

Fix: calculate the limit from the wind at the working altitude, fly out into the wind, descend for the return.

Weather and flight planning: wind, density altitude, cold, icing
In the mountains the aircraft is sluggish and drains the battery fast

Cause: a high density altitude — less thrust, more power to hover.

Fix: recalculate the thrust-to-weight ratio and flight time, remove payload, props suited to the density.

Weather and flight planning: wind, density altitude, cold, icing
In winter, a voltage warning right after takeoff

Cause: a cold battery, high internal resistance.

Fix: warm it to 20–25 °C, hover at low throttle at the start, a shorter flight.

Weather and flight planning: wind, density altitude, cold, icing
In fog, vibration grows and thrust drops

Cause: prop icing.

Fix: land immediately; do not fly in visible moisture around 0 °C.

Weather and flight planning: wind, density altitude, cold, icing
Bumpiness on takeoff and landing in moderate wind

Cause: turbulence behind an obstacle.

Fix: move the takeoff point to the windward side or several obstacle heights away.

Weather and flight planning: wind, density altitude, cold, icing
Video interference in the middle of a sortie

Cause: a neighbor powered up on an adjacent frequency.

Fix: a frequency table, separation, landing and changing the channel if needed.

Field operations and teamwork
Two radios interfere with each other

Cause: the same binding phrase or an overloaded band.

Fix: unique phrases, lower the power when close, separate the flights in time.

Field operations and teamwork
A crashed aircraft is not found

Cause: no last coordinates, the buzzer is not configured.

Fix: BEEPER on a switch, beacon RX_LOST, SD Logs recording on the radio and DVR in the goggles, a GCS recording a tlog; search from the last point forward along the heading, using the pattern from the section “Searching for a crashed aircraft”.

Field operations and teamwork
The pilot got distracted by telemetry and lost sight of the aircraft

Cause: no division of roles.

Fix: the observer and the technician are separate people.

Field operations and teamwork
The analysis of your own crash ends with “I don't know why”

Cause: there was no on-board recording or telemetry.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: nothing to look at.

Consequences: the cause will repeat.

Fix: enable Blackbox/DataFlash on all flights, record a tlog on the GCS.

Analysis of real crashes
The failsafe is “configured” but has never been tested

Cause: no ground test in the procedure.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: no failsafe event in the log over the whole history of the aircraft.

Consequences: as with the Airspeeder — the aircraft does something other than what you think.

Fix: a test without props for every new configuration, the result goes into the test table.

Analysis of real crashes
Battery thresholds lowered “to fly longer”

Cause: the margin was sacrificed for flight time.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the voltage under load near the critical level before the warning triggers.

Consequences: as with the Alta X — a fall in free fall.

Fix: thresholds from the discharge curve of your batteries, not lower than the manufacturer's recommendations.

Analysis of real crashes
Vibration grew on only one IMU

Cause: a local source (mount, sensor) or damage.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: VIBE diverges between the IMUs, the EKF innovations grow.

Consequences: an EKF failsafe, for the Matternet — the FTS.

Fix: land, inspect the props and the mount; an IMU mask that allows a faulty sensor to be isolated.

Analysis of real crashes
After a crash the aircraft “seems intact”, but vibration has grown

Cause: a bent motor shaft or bell, a cracked arm, a damaged flight controller damper, a prop with a microcrack.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: gyro noise and VIBE are higher than in the logs before the crash; one motor is louder in the test without props.

Fix: go through the whole post-crash checklist, replace all the props, spin each motor by hand and in the test without props.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
One battery “dies” faster than the others

Cause: IR growth in one cell, imbalance.

Fix: per-cell IR, the logbook; if it exceeds the criteria — retire it.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
Vibration grows from flight to flight

Cause: bearing wear, a damaged prop, loosened mounting.

Fix: spin the motors by hand, replace the props, check the mounting, compare with the replacement log.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
One motor is hotter than the others after a flight

Cause: motor wear, a heavy/damaged prop, an unbalanced aircraft.

Fix: the motor outputs in the log, replace the prop, check the CG, then the motor.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
A “sudden” failure without warnings

Cause: no logbook and no trends — there were warnings, but nobody recorded them.

Fix: a logbook, trends from the logs, replacement by service life.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
Strange compass behavior after a repair

Cause: the power wires are routed differently, current-induced interference.

Fix: compare MAG against current before and after, recalibrate, restore the wire routing.

Maintenance and reliability: logs, battery life, maintenance schedule, MTBF
“nothing helped, I reflashed it, it works”

Cause: the cause was not found, a coincidence (more often — reseating contacts).

Fix: treat it as unresolved; record it as an open case, keep watching.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
An intermittent problem, appears once every few flights

Cause: a contact/solder joint, vibration, temperature.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: an event in the log (an eRPM dip, a VBAT jump, RXLOSS) at the moment of the symptom.

Fix: a log on every flight; wiggle the harnesses with the aircraft powered; spot heating with a hot-air gun.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
After replacing a part the symptom remained

Cause: the part was not at fault — a branch was eliminated incorrectly.

Fix: go back to the last confirmed fact.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
The aircraft disarmed or shut off in the air

Cause: a failsafe with a “disarm” action, a bumped switch, the crash detector, a power sag with a flight controller reboot.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the disarm reason code (tree 6, step 1); a log cut off without a disarm event — power.

Fix: copy the log before any power-up; follow the branch for your reason; do not fly until it is resolved.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
In a position-holding mode the aircraft drifts sideways or circles

Cause: compass interference, a GPS glitch, a position estimate failure.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: ArduPilot — ERR Subsys 11, 16, 17; the XKF4 variances.

Fix: in the air — switch to a mode without GPS and manual throttle; on the ground — the EKF and GPS branches of tree 6.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
A black screen in digital goggles

Cause: no binding, different firmware versions on the module and goggles, overheating or a power sag on the module, the antenna.

Fix: tree 5, the digital system branch — in order: binding, versions, power, overheating, antenna.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
The drone flips over immediately on takeoff

Cause: the motor order or spin direction is wrong, a wrong mixer.

Fix: without props, check each motor against the frame diagram on the Motors tab.

Troubleshooting tree: the drone won't take off
Желе — прямые линии изгибаются волной

Cause: пропеллер с повреждением или дисбалансом, погнутый вал, подшипник; камера на жёстком креплении к раме; у DJI O3/O4 — ШИМ ESC 24 кГц интерферирует с опросом IMU.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: пик gyroUnfilt на частоте вращения мотора или широкий шум.

Fix: заменить пропы, проверить валы и балансировку (f-frame-mech), TPU-крепление; для O3/O4 — мягкое крепление или Optical correction, ШИМ ESC 48 кГц и выше.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
После Gyroflow горизонт дрейфует или кадр «качается»

Cause: не введён наклон камеры при логе полётника; неверная ориентация IMU; противоречащие точки синхронизации; изменены настройки после синхронизации.

Fix: ввести наклон в Rotation → Pitch, удалить выпадающую точку, пересинхронизировать 2–3 точками на участках с движением.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
Gyroflow не находит гироскоп в файле DJI

Cause: включён RockSteady/EIS или FOV не Wide.

Fix: выключить EIS, FOV Wide; для уже снятого — Optical correction.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
Смаз «дрожит» после стабилизации

Cause: длинная выдержка (180° и ND) при сильном сглаживании.

Fix: 90° или 45°, слабее ND или без него.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
Полосы и мерцание в помещении

Cause: выдержка не кратна периоду пульсаций света (100 Гц при сети 50 Гц).

Fix: 1/100 или 1/50 с, частота кадров 50.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
Запись обрывается или пропадают кадры

Cause: карта медленнее потока (V10 при 120 Мбит/с), заполнена встроенная память (4 ГБ у O4 Pro), перегрев.

Fix: V30 и выше, посчитать объём на сессию, обдув на земле (bf-hd-osd-video).

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
После установки камеры аппарат стал вялым или колеблется по тангажу

Cause: +45–200 г и сдвиг центра масс вперёд-вверх.

Consequences: другая инерция, перегрев моторов, меньше времени полёта.

Fix: Blackbox и перепроверка тюна (bf-pid), сдвинуть батарею.

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
С включённой камерой хуже GPS или срывы управления 2.4 ГГц

Cause: Wi-Fi/Bluetooth камеры, помехи от её электроники и питания.

Fix: выключить беспроводные функции, отнести камеру и провод питания от GPS, сравнить C/N0 (el-emi).

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR
После потери аппарата нечем искать

Cause: писалась только бортовая запись, DVR очков выключен.

Fix: включать DVR очков всегда, координаты в OSD (ops-field).

Съёмка с FPV: экшн-камера, ND-фильтры, Gyroflow и DVR

Electronics and radio

The FC reboots on a sharp throttle punch

Cause: a sag below the BEC threshold or a bad connector contact.

Fix: VBAT log, check the XT connector, the capacitor, a higher-capacity/fresher battery.

Onboard power circuit design
Lines in the analog video on throttle

Cause: power bus ripple gets into the VTX.

Fix: a capacitor on the power bus, power the VTX from a filtered BEC, an LC filter.

Onboard power circuit design
Gyro noise went up after replacing the ESC

Cause: a different switching frequency, no capacitor.

Fix: a capacitor on the pads, check the FC grounding.

Onboard power circuit design
The BEC is hot

Cause: a linear BEC with a high input voltage, or current above the rating.

Fix: recompute the dissipated power, a buck instead of an LDO, offload the branch.

Onboard power circuit design
The receiver loses LQ when the video turns on

Cause: a switching BEC or the VTX next to the receiver antenna.

Fix: separate them, shielding, power the RX from a separate LDO.

Onboard power circuit design
The multimeter shows 5.0 V, but the FC reboots

Cause: a brief sag the multimeter can't see.

Fix: an oscilloscope with a falling-edge trigger, or the log.

Measurements: multimeter, oscilloscope, logic analyzer
The oscilloscope shows huge ripple on everything

Cause: the long probe ground wire picks up noise.

Fix: a ground spring, measure right at the capacitor.

Measurements: multimeter, oscilloscope, logic analyzer
The analyzer shows garbage on UART

Cause: wrong baud rate or inversion (SBUS).

Fix: find the right baud rate, enable inversion in the decoder.

Measurements: multimeter, oscilloscope, logic analyzer
An I2C sensor doesn't respond (NACK)

Cause: no pull-up, a different address, SDA/SCL swapped.

Fix: check the idle levels (they should be high), scan the addresses.

Measurements: multimeter, oscilloscope, logic analyzer
DShot edges ring

Cause: long signal wires, no common ground with the ESC.

Fix: shorten them, add a ground next to the signal, drop DShot to 300.

Measurements: multimeter, oscilloscope, logic analyzer
One group is always lower after a flight

Cause: a weak cell or a bad weld in the group (high R).

Fix: IR per group, re-measure the cells, re-weld.

Li-Ion and LiFePO4 packs: cells, welding, BMS
Heavy sag with normal capacity

Cause: too few P for the current, thin strip, nickel-plated steel.

Fix: recalculate P, use thicker pure nickel strip/parallel paths.

Li-Ion and LiFePO4 packs: cells, welding, BMS
The pack cut out in flight

Cause: the BMS tripped on current/temperature.

Fix: take the BMS off the discharge path, voltage failsafe in the firmware.

Li-Ion and LiFePO4 packs: cells, welding, BMS
70% of the rated capacity in the test

Cause: counterfeit cells, old cells, discharge in the cold.

Fix: test individual cells, buy from trusted suppliers, warm the pack to 20 °C.

Li-Ion and LiFePO4 packs: cells, welding, BMS
The strip comes off the cell with a finger

Cause: a weak weld.

Fix: tune the energy on scraps, 4 welds, check the electrodes.

Li-Ion and LiFePO4 packs: cells, welding, BMS
Heading drifts on throttle, “toilet bowling” in Loiter

Cause: the magnetic field of the power wires at the compass.

Fix: compass on a mast, twist the wires, compassmot/external compass.

Interference and electromagnetic compatibility
Satellites drop out when the companion/camera is turned on

Cause: radiation from USB 3.0/HDMI/buck at 1.5 GHz.

Fix: shielding, ferrites, GPS on a mast, USB 2.0 cables where possible.

Interference and electromagnetic compatibility
LQ drops near the VTX

Cause: wideband VTX noise at the RX antenna.

Fix: separate the antennas, lower VTX power on the ground, a filter on the VTX supply.

Interference and electromagnetic compatibility
Lines in the video on throttle

Cause: ripple and a ground loop.

Fix: LC filter, capacitor, single star ground.

Interference and electromagnetic compatibility
The link drops when the aircraft is directly overhead

Cause: the dipole pattern null along its axis.

Fix: a second antenna at 90°, tilt the radio's antenna.

Antennas, polarization and radiation patterns
Video gets noisy when banking

Cause: linear antennas, polarization mismatch.

Fix: circular polarization on both ends, the same RHCP/LHCP.

Antennas, polarization and radiation patterns
The VTX gets very hot, range is short

Cause: high SWR — an antenna for a different band, or a damaged one.

Fix: check the SWR/replace the antenna; never power the VTX without an antenna.

Antennas, polarization and radiation patterns
RSSI is good, LQ is bad

Cause: noise near the RX antenna, not the antenna itself.

Fix: see the module on interference; move it away from the VTX.

Antennas, polarization and radiation patterns
GPS takes a long time to acquire satellites

Cause: the patch is under carbon, no ground plane, interference nearby.

Fix: a mast, a metal plate under the patch.

Antennas, polarization and radiation patterns
RSSI drops faster than calculated right from the start

Cause: losses in a cable/connector, polarization, an antenna under carbon.

Fix: check SWR, pigtails, orientation.

Radio link budget and range
The link drops at the same range every time even with good RSSI

Cause: onboard interference reduces effective sensitivity; LQ drops earlier.

Fix: watch LQ, see the module on interference.

Radio link budget and range
In the field the margin is 10 dB worse than calculated

Cause: flying low — obstacles in the Fresnel zone, multipath.

Fix: more altitude, a higher ground antenna, a directional antenna.

Radio link budget and range
Video is gone, RC still holds

Cause: the video budget ran out first (expected on 5.8 GHz).

Fix: a patch on the ground, more power, another band; RTH on video loss.

Radio link budget and range
The device doesn't respond, the port is configured

Cause: TX/RX not crossed, speed, inversion, no common ground.

Fix: analyzer on the device's line: are there bytes; swap TX/RX; check GND.

Connecting peripherals: UART, I2C, CAN in practice
An I2C device is “sometimes there, sometimes not”

Cause: long wires, no pull-ups, noise from the power wiring.

Fix: shorter, twisted, 100 kHz, 2.2–4.7 kΩ pull-ups.

Connecting peripherals: UART, I2C, CAN in practice
After connecting a companion the FC reboots

Cause: the flight controller's 5 V BEC is overloaded.

Fix: a separate 5 V/3–5 A BEC for the companion, common ground.

Connecting peripherals: UART, I2C, CAN in practice
A DroneCAN node isn't visible

Cause: no terminators, CAN_H/CAN_L swapped, driver not enabled.

Fix: 120 Ω at the ends, check CAN_P1_DRIVER/CAN_D1_PROTOCOL, reboot.

Connecting peripherals: UART, I2C, CAN in practice
A port burned out after connecting a 5 V sensor

Cause: the pin isn't 5V-tolerant, or power got onto the signal line.

Fix: a divider/level shifter; another port; check the MCU datasheet.

Connecting peripherals: UART, I2C, CAN in practice
A node appears and disappears

Cause: no terminator or three of them, a bad contact in a JST-GH, a star instead of a line.

Fix: 60 Ω between H and L with the power off, check the connectors, rework the topology.

DroneCAN: the onboard CAN bus, nodes, ESCs and peripheral firmware
No nodes visible at all

Cause: CAN_P1_DRIVER or CAN_D1_PROTOCOL not set, no reboot, no 5 V on the bus.

Fix: parameters, reboot, voltage on pin 1 of the connector.

DroneCAN: the onboard CAN bus, nodes, ESCs and peripheral firmware
Two GPS units “flicker”, the data jumps

Cause: a node ID conflict after moving a node from another vehicle.

Fix: clear the DNA table, restore automatic ID allocation.

DroneCAN: the onboard CAN bus, nodes, ESCs and peripheral firmware
CAN motors twitch at high RPM

Cause: bus load close to the limit, the ESC command rate too high for the number of nodes.

Fix: measure the load in DroneCAN GUI Tool, move the ESCs to a separate CAN2 bus.

DroneCAN: the onboard CAN bus, nodes, ESCs and peripheral firmware
The node is visible, but there is no data in the EKF

Cause: the device type is not selected (GPS1_TYPE, BATT_MONITOR, RNGFND1_TYPE).

Fix: set the DroneCAN type for the sensor you need, reboot.

DroneCAN: the onboard CAN bus, nodes, ESCs and peripheral firmware
A motor stutters or cuts out on throttle

Cause: a cold joint on a phase or a chafed wire.

Fix: resolder, continuity test, vibration test.

Soldering, harnesses and connection reliability
The XT60 heats up

Cause: a bad solder joint or oxidized contacts.

Fix: resolder with proper heating, replace the connector.

Soldering, harnesses and connection reliability
Intermittent receiver/GPS dropouts

Cause: a cracked strand at the joint or in a JST crimp.

Fix: wiggle the wire with the aircraft powered, resolder and secure.

Soldering, harnesses and connection reliability
An FC pad lifted

Cause: overheating or a yanked wire.

Fix: solder to the nearest trace/via, secure the wire with glue.

Soldering, harnesses and connection reliability
A short after assembly

Cause: a solder bridge between neighboring pads, a wire touching carbon.

Fix: continuity test before power-up, smoke stopper.

Soldering, harnesses and connection reliability
A hexa spins and falls after a motor failure

Cause: T/W on the remaining motors < 2, no yaw margin.

Fix: more thrust/less mass, MOT_YAW_HEADROOM, an X8 instead of a hexa.

Onboard redundancy: X8, hexa, power and signals
One X8 arm runs hotter

Cause: a wire/connector too thin for the double current.

Fix: 12 AWG, XT90, check the soldering.

Onboard redundancy: X8, hexa, power and signals
The FC rebooted on a current peak

Cause: a single BEC on a sagging bus.

Fix: dual BECs with ORing, capacitors, power the FC from a power module with margin.

Onboard redundancy: X8, hexa, power and signals
RC loss when the antenna turns

Cause: a single receiver without diversity.

Fix: diversity or a second receiver on another band.

Onboard redundancy: X8, hexa, power and signals
One RCOU channel at maximum in the log

Cause: a motor/ESC failed or a prop broke.

Fix: land; check the ESC telemetry RPM for that motor.

Onboard redundancy: X8, hexa, power and signals
The current reading has a constant error that grows with current

Cause: no Kelvin measurement — the drop across the copper gets into the reading.

Fix: a sense pair from the shunt pads; refine the calibration against a reference.

PCB design in KiCad: from schematic to order, layout for EMC
The device isn't found on I²C

Cause: wrong address via A0/A1, no pull-ups, SDA/SCL swapped in the connector.

Fix: check the address, the pull-ups, the pinout against Dronecode.

PCB design in KiCad: from schematic to order, layout for EMC
The shunt or traces get very hot

Cause: underestimated I²R power, narrow traces, too little copper.

Fix: a higher-power package or parallel shunts, pours, 2 oz, wires to the pads.

PCB design in KiCad: from schematic to order, layout for EMC
After installing the board the compass jumps or the gyro gets noisy

Cause: power currents and a converter next to the sensors, a slot in the ground.

Fix: separate them, short current loops, solid ground, compass on a mast.

PCB design in KiCad: from schematic to order, layout for EMC
The chip doesn't fit on the board

Cause: wrong footprint (package, pin pitch).

Fix: check the footprint against the datasheet before ordering; print the board at 1:1 and lay the components on it.

PCB design in KiCad: from schematic to order, layout for EMC

Конструктор

Цикл в скрипте не сходится или прыгает между 6S3P и 6S4P

Cause: целое число параллелей; при большой доле рамы или тяжёлом грузе масса растёт быстрее энергии (решения нет).

Fix: зафиксировать n_p и пройти таблицу времени; если время не растёт с nP — менять диаметр пропа или схему, а не АКБ.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
Ток висения в логе на 20–30 % выше прогноза

Cause: реальная масса больше бюджета; таблица мотора снята с другим пропом или напряжением; датчик тока не откалиброван.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: BAT.Curr выше, CTUN.ThO выше ожидаемого.

Fix: взвесить аппарат, проверить BATT_AMP_PERVLT клещами, пересчитать FM по факту.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
Ток висения совпадает, а время полёта на 25 % меньше

Cause: холодная АКБ, ёмкость ниже номинала, отсечка выше заложенной (снимается меньше 85 %).

Fix: проверить ёмкость циклом на зарядке, уменьшить usable в модели, греть АКБ зимой.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
MOT_THST_HOVER выше 0.5

Cause: T/W ниже 2 — модель занизила массу или завысила тягу на просевшей АКБ.

Consequences: нет запаса на ветер и стабилизацию к концу разряда.

Fix: проп большего диаметра, мотор с запасом или облегчение; повторить расчёт.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
В ветер аппарат не держит точку и ложится на предельный угол

Cause: CdA занижен, ограничение наклона ATC_ANGLE_MAX достигнуто.

Fix: оценить CdA по углу наклона в известный ветер (tg θ = D/mg), пересчитать запас, ограничить работу по ветру.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
Моторы горячие после висения

Cause: ток висения выше ~40 % длительного тока мотора.

Fix: проверить по таблице рабочую точку, взять мотор крупнее или проп с меньшим шагом.

Проектирование под задачу: ТЗ → масса, энергия, тяга, конфигурация
Редкие огромные скачки тяги (тысячи граммов) или значения около ±8388608

Cause: прерывание (Wi-Fi, другой ISR) растянуло импульс PD_SCK дольше 60 мкс — HX711 ушёл в power-down или сдвинул биты.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: одиночные выбросы на ровном сигнале.

Fix: чтение в portENTER_CRITICAL, Wi-Fi выключить на время прогона, отбрасывать отсчёты на границах шкалы.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
При постоянной нагрузке показания медленно растут, ноль после прогона не совпадает с нулём до

Cause: ползучесть балки и температурный уход нуля (±0.2 % FS на 10 °C — до ±10 г у балки 5 кг).

Fix: прогрев 15 мин, нуль до и после, линейная поправка по времени, короткие ступени.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
Тяга со знаком минус или растёт при снятии гири

Cause: перепутаны A+ и A− или балка стоит вверх ногами; под мотором — перевёрнут проп или неверное вращение.

Fix: поменять провода сигнала или знак k, проверить проп и направление.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
Тяга на 10–20 % выше паспорта при нормальном токе

Cause: калибровали в другой точке приложения силы (ошибка плеча), или блок с трением съел часть веса гирь; проп близко к полу или стене.

Fix: калибровать вдоль оси пропа, проверить гистерезис, отодвинуть стенд на 1–2 D.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
C_T совпадает с паспортом, а г/Вт ниже на 5–15 %

Cause: потери в приводе — другой ESC и тайминг, тонкие или длинные провода, напряжение меряется на БП, а не на ESC.

Consequences: время висения будет меньше паспортного расчёта в той же пропорции.

Fix: мерить U на входе ESC, сравнить с ESC из таблицы, проверить сечения.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
Обороты вдвое выше ожидаемых или C_T скачет от точки к точке

Cause: оптодатчик ловит две метки или блики от лопастей, неверный TACH_PPR или делитель полюсов для eRPM.

Fix: одна матовая метка, экран от света, сверка с внешним тахометром.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
Мотор продолжает крутиться, когда завис ПК или отвалился датчик

Cause: безопасность привязана к циклу чтения датчика или нет таймаута команды.

Consequences: неуправляемый проп 15" — травма.

Fix: отдельный таймер безопасности, таймаут 0.5 с, аппаратный выключатель питания ESC.

Тяговый стенд: тензодатчик + HX711, ток, обороты, КПД
После броска самолёт задирает нос и сваливается, руль высоты в упоре вниз

Cause: ЦТ позади нейтральной точки (SM < 0) — частая ошибка, когда АКБ сдвинута назад ради «лёгкого хвоста» или летающее крыло отцентровано по четверти корневой хорды.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: CTUN.Pitch растёт против CTUN.NavPitch, выход руля высоты в RCOU на пределе, PIDP.I сильно отрицательный.

Fix: пересчитать NP, сдвинуть ЦТ вперёд на 5–10 % САХ, повторить балансировку.

Самолёт: устойчивость, центровка, статический запас, поляра
На крейсере постоянно нужен руль вверх, посадка «плюхом», не хватает выравнивания

Cause: тяжёлый нос, SM > 20 %.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: устойчиво положительный PIDP.I, руль высоты заметно выше SERVOn_TRIM.

Fix: сдвинуть ЦТ назад на 3–5 мм за итерацию до запаса 10 %, затем заново выставить триммер.

Самолёт: устойчивость, центровка, статический запас, поляра
Раскачка по тангажу в FBWA/AUTO, AUTOTUNE выдал очень маленькие PTCH_RATE_*
